## Executive Finding

Exit status is `2`: multiple reachable artifacts claim canonical or live authority. The repo should not start another broad research-generation pass until normal retrieval is constrained to the canonical core and stale/generated outputs are excluded by default.

The confirmed restart foundation for PAGC-derived applications should be treated as:

```text
source-observed layer: 26 rows x 8 vowel/modifier columns = 208 records
derived-operational layer: 27 / 216 only when the f/v row is split
research target: applications and systems that preserve the source/derived distinction
```
